```text
Excellent! Llama C4 hires cross-dataset results are clear:

**Key results**:
- **C4 eval**: MCKP PPL = 7.5351, Uniform PPL = 7.5351, **Delta = 0.0000** → **FAIL**
- **WT2 eval** (C4-calibrated): MCKP = 8.5103, Uniform = 8.4701, Delta = -0.0402 → FAIL (MCKP worse)


- **WT2-cal reference**: MCKP = 8.5042, Uniform = 8.4701, Delta = -0.0341 → FAIL

The FAIL verdict holds across datasets—MCKP shows zero improvement on C4 and actually performs worse on WT2, confirming this isn't a WikiText-2 artifact. The spike distribution at L2H5 simply doesn't give the solver enough leverage at k=2, which is exactly what Director wanted to demonstrate.

On C4, MCKP and uniform converge to nearly identical perplexity (7.5351) because the allocation is almost entirely 4-bit, making the two approaches functionally equivalent. Now I'm updating the registry to mark llama_c4_hires_crossdataset as negative, then reporting back to Director before continuing with Gemma-2.
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I need to update the notes with the full analysis and report to Director. The key finding is:

Llama C4 hires cross-dataset: FAIL confirmed
- C4 eval: MCKP = Uniform = 7.5351 (delta = 0.000)
- WT2 eval (C4-calibrated): MCKP = 8.5103, Uniform = 8.4701 (delta = -0.040, MCKP worse)
- Bit distribution identical: {4:253, 2:2, 8:1}


- This confirms the FAIL verdict holds across datasets

I notice the script ran Phase 1 hires profiling on C4 data despite passing --reuse-hires, so it appears the flag either didn't work as intended or the script treats C4 hires profiling as a separate step that can't be reused from WT2.

Looking at the results, the 2-bit profiles came from C4-specific hires profiling while the 4/8-bit results were reused from prior runs, which actually strengthens the conclusion since everything was evaluated in-domain on C4 data.

cuda:1 is now free again, and Director wanted to reserve it for Mistral C4 after Gemma-2 finishes on cuda:0, but since Gemma-2 is still running, cuda:1 is sitting idle for now.

I should report back to Director with the Llama C4 results confirming the cross-dataset failure, note that cuda:1 is available and waiting for instructions, and give an update on Gemma-2's progress.
```

_source line 398 · 2026-05-14T22:52:33.628Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01McWWs2bc6ssq833TVAsBVg`</summary>

```text
65	\begin{enumerate}[nosep]
66	\item Run per-head profiling as described in \S\ref{sec:profiling}.
67	\item Compute the top-5\% concentration and the $t_1/t_5$ ratio (top-1 distortion divided by top-5 distortion) from the resulting distortion table.
68	\item Select allocation strategy based on concentration \emph{and} shape:
69	  \begin{itemize}[nosep]
70	  \item \textbf{Spread} (concentration $\gtrsim 0.50$, $t_1/t_5 \lesssim 0.70$): non-uniform allocation recommended. Multiple heads exhibit cross-bitwidth sensitivity gradients, giving the solver room to redistribute budget. Qwen (0.61, $t_1/t_5{=}0.57$): 27.3\% PPL improvement.
71	  \item \textbf{Spike} ($t_1/t_5 \gtrsim 0.70$, any concentration): use uniform. Sensitivity is dominated by a single outlier with no cross-bitwidth gradient; the solver cannot exploit it. Yi-1.5 (0.71, $t_1/t_5{=}0.90$) and Llama (0.57, $t_1/t_5{=}0.85$): no meaningful benefit.
72	  \item \textbf{Flat} (concentration $\lesssim 0.50$): use uniform. Near-uniform sensitivity provides no signal for differentiated allocation. When concentration falls below 0.50, the top-5\% heads contribute too little cumulative delta for the solver to construct a meaningfully non-uniform allocation; consequently the $t_1/t_5$ ratio---regardless of its value---does not alter the outcome, and concentration alone suffices to determine the category. Mistral (0.24, $t_1/t_5{=}0.41$) and Gemma-2 (0.23, $t_1/t_5{=}0.18$): counterproductive.
73	  \end{itemize}
74	\end{enumerate}
75	
76	Llama-3.1-8B illustrates why both conditions are needed: its concentration (0.57) lies above the 0.50 threshold and would be misclassified as beneficial by concentration alone, but its spike shape ($t_1/t_5{=}0.85$) correctly predicts no meaningful benefit.
77	Indeed, a concentration-only threshold would misclassify two of the three high-concentration models (Yi-1.5 at 0.71 and Llama at 0.57), making the $t_1/t_5$ shape condition essential for avoiding false positives.
78	The joint condition---concentration $\gtrsim 0.50$ and $t_1/t_5 \lesssim 0.70$---correctly separates the beneficial case from the four non-beneficial cases across all five architectures studied.
79	Both thresholds are illustrative, derived from five models; they should be validated on a broader set before being treated as general rules.
80	The diagnostic adds no computational overhead: it reuses the profiling data already required for the allocation solver and avoids wasted optimization effort on architectures where non-uniform allocation provides no benefit.
81	Additional analyses---the effect of aggressive compression ratios, profiling resolution and multi-seed stability, per-layer optimality validation, and KV separation ablation---are provided in Appendices~\ref{sec:aggressive}--\ref{sec:kv_separation}.
82	
```

### Assistant

_source line 450 · 2026-05-14T22:56:08.770Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
CUDA error: invalid device ordinal. The issue is that `submit_training_job` sets `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1` (for cuda:1), which means only GPU 1 is visible. But the script internally might try to use a specific device like `cuda:1` when only `cuda:0` is visible (since CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 makes physical GPU 1 appear as cuda:0).

Looking at the script - it probably has a hardcoded device reference. The `run_mistral_c4_cross_dataset.py` likely has `cuda:1` or some other device specification. 

So the remapping means physical GPU 1 becomes cuda:0 inside the process, and I need to check what device references are actually in the script.
```

</details>

```json
  "content": "[WRITE HANDOFF] 请立即写你的 handoff。\n\n**格式硬约束**:\n- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹\n- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（\"好的\"/\"我开始写\"/\"写完了\"等）\n\n**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:\n\n### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线\n  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。\n  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。\n  下一个\"你\"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。\n\n### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照\n  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env\n  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache\n  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID\n  写一次就稳定，下一个\"你\"不用再 SSH 探查。\n\n### 3. 📊 当前状态\n  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么\n  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要\n  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策\n\n### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）\n  格式 \"条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y\"。例如:\n  - \"Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交\"\n  - \"pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result\"\n\n### 5. 🎯 战略上下文\n  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）\n  - 下一个\"你\"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑\n\n**写作原则**：\n- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）\n- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查\n- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff\n\n示例格式:\n&lt;handoff>\n### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线\n- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）\n- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）\n\n### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照\n- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases\n- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）\n- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct\n- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)\n\n### 3. 📊 当前状态\n我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。\nregistry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。\n\n### 4. 📋 待办\n- pilot_011 cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 probe + GC4 计算\n- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪\n\n### 5. 🎯 战略上下文\nFalcon-H1R 是最后一个数据点。已知 4B/9B GC4 都 FAIL。\n易错：不要把 GC4-par (Falcon parallel) 和 GC4-seq (Qwen sequential) 直接对比。\n&lt;/handoff>"
```

  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
